**Thesis (v9, 2026-10-20, loop C round C8, nodes P34/P34v, P36/P36v, P37 (not run); v1–v8 above kept):** v8 kept, with
two changes:
1. **Axis moment at natural offset sizes (new C18, 0.5; C3 0.9 kept):** recentring jets whose pT-weighted centre sits
   naturally off the stored axis, at fixed invariant mass, moves ParT_full's W-vs-Z log-odds as a real mass change of the
   same ½Δlog M2 does (R 1.05 [0.74, 1.40] on 800 most off-centre W/Z jets, 0.94 [0.83, 1.06] on all W/Z jets; ParticleNet
   0.7–0.8). The mass sense is measured about the stored axis, not about the pT centroid, also for offsets of the size
   real jets have (median 0.03 rad in the top 5 %, a ≈ 0.7 % change of √M2). The recentred jets are artificial axis shifts
   of natural size (their momentum direction moves 0.026 rad off the stored axis); unedited jets are not tested.
2. **Lepton class (C15 v2, 0.55; v1 wording kept in the claim list):** the e/μ flag dependence replicates on two fresh
   samples with a clean matched-pT control (97.8–98.7 % vs 0.3–1.3 %; e↔μ 0.07–0.2 %), and a muon flag on a hadron in a
   no-lepton jet creates the H→ℓνqq′ call in 18 % of jets (control 0.16 %). A literal circuit of the lepton-reading head
   b2h1 keeps a lepton-presence score (AUC 0.92) but not the call; in the intact network the class-block MLPs are not
   needed for the call and the particle-block MLPs are needed to keep leptonic tops out. The units that make the call in
   the intact network are not identified. The top-quark head circuit (C17) is pre-registered and untested.

**Thesis log (round C8, 2026-10-20).**
- P34 thesis (C15: a literal circuit of the lepton heads alone keeps the H→ℓνqq′ call above random head sets): flag
  dependence and redundancy supported on two fresh samples (e1 98.7 % / 97.8 % vs e2n 1.3 % / 0.7 %; class heads 12.6–13.3 %,
  particle heads 5.8–6.9 %, all 95.1–95.7 %; b2h1 + k random heads +3 to +28 %). "Literal circuit keeps the call" not
  supported: argmax recall 0 for every literal head circuit (constant call); score AUC 0.92; the plan's alternative ("heads
  route, MLPs compute") was P34's reading, and P34v limits it to the reduced network: with all 80 heads kept, the class-block
  MLPs at their mean leave recall 0.980 → 0.975, while the particle-block MLPs at their mean raise t→bℓν → H→ℓνqq′ from
  0.010 to 0.136. Top-k AUC advantage over random sets not robust (p 0.010 → 0.082); specificity sits in b2h1 (the only class
  head that gives recall with the class MLPs kept). New sufficiency edit (P34v): 0.16 → 18.3 %. C15 v1 wording kept, v2
  (0.5 → 0.55).
- P36 thesis (exploratory; axis-relative read-out acts on naturally off-centre jets, R_nat ≈ 0.7–0.9; on-manifold
  invariance as alternative): R_nat 1.33 (tail5) / 1.08 (all W/Z), P36v 1.05 / 0.94; on-manifold-invariance alternative not
  supported. P36's "acts on natural jets, not only on artificial shifts" scoped by P36v: recentring moves the momentum axis
  0.026 rad off the stored axis, so it is an artificial axis shift of natural size and direction. P36's "R_nat equals the
  random-shift R" holds on all W/Z jets (0.94 vs 0.82, p 0.17), not on the tail (1.05 vs 0.35, p 0.007). P36's ParT_kin
  "opposite sign as P23 predicts" holds for the probe only, not the log-odds read-out. New C18 (0.5); C3 (0.9) kept with a
  note; C6 (0.0) unchanged (L-GATr interim R 0.2–0.3, not verified; P6 is the pre-registered test).
- P37 (top-quark head circuit, new C17): not run; three smoke launches rejected at admission on node k8s-haosu-09 ("GPU is
  lost"). part_job.sh now excludes the node (commit c6cf2c4). C17 entered as a pre-registered claim (0.0).
- Method: literal circuits report AUC and a threshold read-out next to argmax recall (mean ablation shifts logits, so argmax
  can be constant while the score separates); necessity of a unit is tested in the intact network as well as in the reduced
  one; rotation about the axis, the φ mirror and the 180° flip are not nulls for the full-input models (10–20 % of calls);
  the φ mirror is a near-null for the kin models only; recentring edits report the momentum-axis offset they create.
- Proposed: P38 (which units make the H→ℓνqq′ call and reject t→bℓν in the intact network; per-neuron ablation of the
  particle- and class-block MLPs), P39 (unedited-jet test of the natural offset; which input makes the full models
  φ-orientation dependent; L-GATr final checkpoint); P37 rerun; P35 still proposed. Still running: anrunw-ai-c-p31-l
  (P31 5×-steps arm), anrunw-ai-c-p5-lgatr2 (L-GATr); anrunw-ai-c-p31-m10 finished (analysis next round).

**Thesis (v10, 2026-10-20, loop C round C9, nodes P37/P37v, P39/P39v, P40/P40v, P31 final; v1–v9 above kept):** v9
kept, with four changes:
1. **Axis moment, edits only (C18 v2, 0.65; v1 wording kept):** under rigid shifts at fixed invariant mass, ParT_full's
   W/Z response follows the particles' spread about the stored axis (M2, or equivalently the pT-centroid offset; rigid
   shifts cannot separate the two), not the displacement of the momentum direction from the stored axis (iso shift
   0.06–0.19 of the recentring response; a perp shift that raises M2 −0.75). On unedited jets the natural offset is set by
   the jet's |η| and width (c_η ≈ −tanh|η_jet| M2/(2 M0)), so its effect on real calls is inferred, not observed. The
   full models' φ-mirror dependence comes from the joint sign of Δφ, charge and d0 (new C20, 0.7), not from the axis.
2. **Top-quark heads (C17 v2, 0.6; C17s 0.3):** five class-token heads carry 59 % of ParT_full's top-vs-QCD drop (class-
   matched random sets 21 %) and 54–66 % of the response to merging a prong; kept alone they do not give the top call.
   Whether the class head sets are class-specific is mixed (joint knock-out mainly lowers top; single-head sets overlap).
3. **Class-token MLP neurons (C19 0.1; C19r 0.45):** as a basis, the neurons are about as readable as random rotations;
   a pre-registered high-impact minority is causal, including axis-mass units that respond to a φ shift as to a real
   mass change (R_W 0.17–0.28, net). Full maps need feature-level units (P13).
4. **Training (C10 v5, 0.65):** none of the tested training changes (10 classes, 5M jets, 5× steps) brings our ParT_kin
   above R ≈ 0.42; the released models' 0.72–0.87 is not reproduced.

**Thesis log (round C9, 2026-10-20).**
- P37 thesis (C17: ≤ 10 heads carry > 50 % of the top drop; a k ≤ 10 literal circuit beats ≥ 95 % of random sets; low
  overlap across classes): concentration supported (top-5 0.58–0.59; kin top-10 0.81); P37v replaced the random-of-80
  null (0.05) by class-matched (0.21) and block-matched (0.33) nulls, which the ranked set still beats (p 0.005–0.01). The
  literal circuit with all MLPs ablated is not above class-matched sets (p 0.12–0.22); with the class-block MLPs kept it is
  (p 0.03–0.04) but top recall ≤ 0.12–0.14. P37's "heads not specific to the top class" contradicted for the joint
  knock-out (z −3.2 to −3.6, Holm 0.04–0.06) and kept for the single-head overlaps (Jaccard 0.20–0.50). The b-prong merge
  acts about twice as strongly as matched W-prong merges (a − b −2.9 to −3.4), and the same heads carry every merge. C17 v1
  0.0 → 0.25; new C17 v2 (0.6) and C17s (0.3).
- P39 thesis (C18 on unedited jets, R_nat 0.7–1.2): not identifiable either way (natural offset collinear with |η_jet|;
  L-GATr shows the same gap; η transport removes 87–172 % of it). The planned rebuilt-axis edit is degenerate (restores the
  offset). The added iso and perp edits exclude momentum-axis displacement as the source of P36's R ≈ 1 (P39v iso/rec 0.06
  [−0.04, 0.14], perp/rec −0.75). P39's "about 0.2 of the response" corrected to 0.06–0.19 (consistent with 0). C18 v2
  (0.65); C3 0.9 kept; new C20 (0.7, mirror sign pattern).
- P31 final (P39 d): pre-registered Alt 3 holds (m10 0.35–0.42, L 0.29–0.35 vs released 0.72–0.87). C10 v5 (0.65).
- P40 thesis (C19: ≥ 2× rotations, > 70 % steering): excluded on two samples. P40's "steering no different from the
  rotated control in ParT_full" not reproduced (P40v 0.583 vs 0.380, p 1e-6; ParT_kin none). The kin deficit in the
  selective fraction is a threshold effect (among clean units neurons beat all 10 rotation seeds). Mass-unit R is a net,
  0.19 of 0.28 in two units. New C19 (0.1) and C19r (0.45).
- Method: random-set nulls are matched to the tested set's block composition (class-token vs particle heads); natural-pair
  comparisons report the |η_jet| balance and an η-transport control; patching ratios R are reported with the oracle set
  (most-shifted units); unit atlases are compared with random rotations at equal absolute step; post-hoc subsets are
  re-tested pre-registered on a fresh sample.
- Proposed: P41 (class-token-only head maps with class-matched nulls; which head reads the b-prong), P42 (axis-mass units
  on a third sample and in a transcoder basis), P43 (non-rigid edits to separate M2 from the centroid offset); P35, P38
  still proposed. Still running: anrunw-ai-c-p5-lgatr2 (L-GATr; interim it154999 84.27 %, P6 after it ends).

**Thesis (v11, 2026-10-21, loop C round C10, nodes P43/P43v, P44/P44v, P45/P45v; v1–v10 above kept):** v10 kept, with
three changes:
1. **Axis moment, both parts (C18 v3, 0.6; v1 and v2 wording kept):** with compensated two-particle edits, ParT_full
   responds to a change of the spread about a fixed pT centroid at 0.86–0.97 of a real mass change, and to a rigid move of
   the centroid off the stored axis (unedited jets, lab four-vectors fixed) at 0.73–0.91, flat in size from 0.005 to
   0.06 rad, so it reads M2 about the stored axis with the offset term counted at about 0.75–0.9 of the spread term.
   ParticleNet_full counts the offset less (0.20–0.61), depending on direction and size.
2. **Shared early features (new C21, 0.55; A8 evidence, row owned by loop A/B, value unchanged here):** ParticleNet's
   per-particle features after its first or second EdgeConv layer stand in for ParT's tokens at blocks 1–3 through one
   affine map (gap 1–2 points; untrained ParticleNet 9–13), its third layer does not. The axis-response size depends on
   donor features and recipient layers together; ParticleNet's smaller response is associated with its first two EdgeConv
   layers plus a short read-out, not with its last EdgeConv layer, pooling or head (C5 kept at 0.75 with this note).
3. **Equivariant contrast (new C22, 0.55; C6 0.0, untested):** in our L-GATr (interim checkpoint) the class decision
   runs through Lorentz-invariant channels with two overlapping copies (grade 0 and the auxiliary scalars); a beam boost
   moves 2.6–2.8 % of calls, only through the time reference and the log-energy inputs. The axis test at fixed invariant
   mass (C6) waits for the final checkpoint.

**Thesis log (round C10, 2026-10-21).**
- P43 thesis (pre-registered: R(E1) and R(E2) both near 1 under C18; R(E2) near 0 under a centroid-relative read-out): the
  spread part holds (ParT_full E1 0.92–0.97 on two samples, E5 null 0.03–0.10). P43's offset weight 0.31 (H1a over H0,
  Holm p 0.002) refuted by P43v: it was measured on E3-deformed jets; on unedited jets the rigid offset gives 0.73 (random
  directions) – 0.91 (E2 direction), flat in dose. P43's suggested dose dependence excluded for ParT_full, present for
  PN_full (0.73 → 0.47). P39v's raise < lower asymmetry not seen in P43 (+0.03); in P43v E1 lower 0.86 < raise 0.97. New
  C18 v3 (0.6); C18 v2 (0.65) kept as the rigid-shift statement; C3 0.9 kept.
- P44 thesis (proposed C21: grade-0 read-out, frame dependence only via spurions): grade-0 part excluded as worded
  (grade 0 alone ≤ 4 points), refined by P44v to redundancy between grade 0 and the auxiliary scalars (g0 after s 6–43
  points). P44's "vector / axial-vector channels carry most early" not upheld (equal to random equal-size sets). Frame
  dependence via the time reference and log-E inputs (Δη/ΔR feature changes 0.16–0.30 %). P44's "C6 supported" not
  taken (no M2-changing iso-mass edit). Mass read-out better in the trained net than untrained (R² 0.91–0.98 vs
  0.76–0.86; P44 said "just as readable"). New C22 (0.55); C6 0.0.
- P45 thesis (H1: ec2/ec3 → b1–b3 within 2 points, far above untrained; R follows PN when stitched late): accuracy holds
  for ec1/ec2 (0.85–2.09; reproduced 1.08–1.34 on fresh jets), fails for ec3 (4.8–8.7). P45's readings "untrained
  features also give a ParT-sized R at early blocks", "ParT's later blocks set R from any angle-carrying features" and
  "PN's smaller response comes from EC3, pooling, fc" refuted by P45v's paired donor comparisons and reverse stitches.
  New C21 (0.55; P45's "C20" id already used); C5 0.75 kept with a note; A8 (loop A/B row) not changed here.
- Method: contrasts are measured on unedited jets with numerator and denominator on the same jets; stitching statements
  need reverse stitches and accuracy-matched comparisons; channel-group ablations report pairwise interactions and
  type-matched controls; exact-symmetry checks are labelled implementation checks.
- Proposed: P46 (= P6, C6 on the final L-GATr checkpoint: M2-changing iso-mass edits), P47 (which sub-step of PN's
  EdgeConv-1/2 lowers R; kin models; other read-outs), P48 (PN_full's direction-dependent offset weight; ParT_full E1
  raise/lower on a third sample); P35, P38, P41, P42 still proposed. Still running: anrunw-ai-c-p5-lgatr2 (L-GATr; 40 h,
  latest checkpoint it174999 of 200,000).

## Paper D: physics journal

**Thesis (v1, 2026-10-16):** physics-motivated input edits on JEDI-linear and ParT, compared with classical taggers at
their published working points, show which physical quantities each model uses and which it does not. Existing
evidence: J25–J33, G192–G206.

**Thesis log (round D1, 2026-10-19, nodes H1–H3).** v1 wording kept. Scope extended: the edits now cover nine models on two
datasets (H1), with one-signal datasets (Top Landscape, QG) set up and models in training (H2).
- D1 (v1 wording kept: "JEDI: mass windows plus a ΣpT shortcut; no W-mass constraint inside top"). H3/H3v: reproduced at 10×
  statistics; extended: against a control that holds the W pair at its mass, neither JEDI-linear nor ParT_full nor ParT_kin
  responds to the W-pair mass inside top (Holm p ≥ 0.13); ParT's top call drops under any rearrangement of the subjet-pair
  masses at fixed jet mass (up to −16 points at 120 GeV). H3's proposed refinement "ParT_full depends a little on the W-pair
  mass (±2.7 points)" was refuted by H3v and is not adopted.
- D2 (v1 wording kept: "ParT: displaced-track multiplicity, not lifetime; isolation used; π⁰, dead cone, lepton–b charge link
  not used"). H3v sharpens the first clause: ParT_full's H→bb call follows the number of tracks with impact-parameter
  significance S ≳ 2–3 (capping every S at 3.5 keeps the count and costs 3.1 points; keeping 2 displaced tracks costs 61). The
  old "lifetime × 0.35" evidence did not hold the count fixed (S > 2 count −26 %). Small H3 effects not re-verified: lepton
  charge in H→ℓνqq′ (−2.6 points), H→cc dead cone (−2.1).
- D3 (was "not started"): done by H3 (verifier confidence 0.45).
- D5 (new, H1/H1v, 0.55): trained taggers (JEDI-linear, Deep Sets, Linformer, transformer, ParT, ParticleNet) are not IRC safe:
  an exact collinear split changes 1–5 % of their calls and none of an IRC-safe EFN's; in-distribution edits (more splits,
  removing the softest particle, count restore) move calls along the particle count. The soft-particle (IR) probe is below
  the data pT floor and position dependent; for ParT_kin / PN_kin it grows as the particle gets softer.
- D6, D7 (new, setup in H2, 0.0): top W-pair mass use; QG constituent counting (Casimir ratio measured 1.59).

**Thesis log (round D2, 2026-10-19, nodes H4, H5, H8).** v1 thesis wording kept; scope now three datasets (hls4ml,
JetClass, Top Landscape) plus quark–gluon, with LorentzNet, PFN and EFN added to the model set.
- D1 (wording kept). Scope stated: the "no W-mass constraint inside top" result holds for JEDI-linear (and ParT, H3v) on
  hls4ml / JetClass. It does not extend to taggers trained on Top Landscape (H4/H4v, D6).
- D6 (setup wording, H2: "top taggers do / do not use the W-pair mass inside top"). New wording (H4/H4v, 0.7): ParT,
  ParticleNet and LorentzNet trained on Top Landscape use the W-pair mass inside top beyond total mass and τ32: at fixed m and
  τ32 they respond 2.1–4.1× a side-effect classifier without the pair mass and as much as the same classifier with it, on two
  samples. H4's thesis note also listed PFN and EFN and called the EFN "not a zero control"; H4v found both at the
  side-effect level, so that part is not adopted (open, H9).
- D7 (setup wording, H2: "quark–gluon taggers count constituents"; H5 pre-registration: "Δlogit per ΔN equals the
  multiplicity-LR prediction 0.46 at fixed IRC-safe shapes, EFN zero"). Not supported except the EFN zero. New wording
  (H5/H5v, 0.7): QG-trained ParT, ParticleNet and LorentzNet do not count constituents (on-manifold added soft particle
  −0.024 to −0.004 vs LR(N) +0.097); their collinear-split response follows the hard-particle energy sharing (ΔN = 0
  control equal). H5's suggested attribution to pT^D is not adopted (H5v: not isolated). Casimir-meets-Poisson
  overpredicts the data LR(N) slope 4.8× because measured multiplicity is about twice as wide as Poisson.
- D5 (wording kept, scope stated): the IRC-unsafe half extends to QG models (H5); the multiplicity half is measured on
  hls4ml / JetClass only, and QG-trained models do not show it.
- D8 (new, H8/H8v, 0.5): an EFP/ADO-chosen IRC-safe surrogate matches the taggers' rankings on natural jets but not their
  edit responses; for JEDI-linear the axis response is reproduced by the axis-moment mass m_A (A4), so the axis gap is mostly
  one known variable. H8's thesis note "the edit responses go beyond what the IRC-safe shape literature describes" is
  narrowed by H8v to: the collinear-split and pT-scaling responses and ParT's Z/W ranking are not described by the pool.
- D4m 0.45 → 0.5 (H8v m_A surrogate, JEDI-linear).

**Thesis log (round D3, 2026-10-19, nodes H12, H13, H14).** v1 thesis wording kept. Two new physics edits (an exact boost
along the jet axis; pileup-like diffuse soft contamination) applied to 20+ models on hls4ml, JetClass, Top Landscape and QG,
and the QG edits repeated on Herwig 7.1 jets.
- D9 (new, H12/H12v, 0.55): hls4ml-trained taggers are not invariant under a boost along the jet axis that fixes m and m_A
  (21–38 % of calls at pT × 0.9 vs 0.3–4.2 % for a matched rotation); JetClass and Top ParT / ParticleNet (and Top
  LorentzNet) change 1.5–4× fewer calls under it than under pT-only or angle-only scaling. H12's proposed explanation
  ("mass read through opening angles at the training pT; wide-pT or four-vector training gives an invariant-mass reading")
  not adopted (H12v: Top models were also trained in a narrow pT window on equivalent inputs; cause untested, H16). H12's
  "Lowering pT moves JEDI calls to the heavier class, opposite of a mass reading" withdrawn (H12v: the flow is W/Z → t, the
  ΣpT shortcut; at the W/Z boundary Δ_B = Δ_P + Δ_A). H12's "supports D4m/A4" not adopted (B fixes m_A too).
- D1 (v1 wording kept). Refined: the D1/H3 "boost × 0.9" (W 87 → 27 %) was a first-order mass-preserving boost, not a
  pT-only edit; an exact pT-only scaling gives W 87.3 → 47.9 %. Weakness 7 (ΣpT shortcut as a sampling artefact): the
  flat-pT retrain lowers the pT-only response (9.6 → 5.8 %) and keeps a 21 % boost response; partly addressed.
- D10 (new, H13/H13v, 0.4): under diffuse soft contamination no network acts like a groomed observable. H13's proposed
  wording ("JEDI-linear acts like the ungroomed m window, JetClass/Top networks between ungroomed and groomed; the response
  comes from the added soft momentum and mass", 0.6) refuted except for JEDI-linear: H13v content controls show that
  JetClass ParT/PN_full and Top ParT/LorentzNet/PFN respond mainly to the number of added soft particles.
- D4 (wording kept, scope): pileup-sized axis offsets (2.5–13 mrad) change 0–3.3 % of calls; the decision-level statement
  applies to WTA / hardest-particle-sized offsets (40–95 mrad), as already scoped after E23v.
- D5 0.55 → 0.6: the multiplicity half gains an on-manifold-like count control on JetClass (ParT/PN_full) and extends to
  Top-trained ParT/LorentzNet/PFN (H13v c4); the QG exception (D7) stands.
- D7 (wording kept, 0.7, H14 not yet verified): the no-counting result holds on Herwig 7.1 test jets; counting plus
  IRC-safe shapes (EFP+N GBDT) excluded by the ΔN = 0 control; the leading-particle fraction z1 at fixed pT^D excluded as
  the hard-core function; pT^D not separated from angular energy flow. H10 absorbed into H14. Herwig-trained models running.
- D4m (0.5, =): no change from H12/H13 (neither isolates the axis-convention response).

**Thesis log (round D4, 2026-10-20, nodes H16, H18, H19).** v1 thesis wording kept. The three open questions of round D3
(a: boost sensitivity, dataset or architecture; b: soft-count response, edit or model; c: W-pair mass, JetClass vs Top)
were tested with matched edits across datasets.
- D7 (wording of 2026-10-19 kept: "QG-trained ParT, ParticleNet, LorentzNet do not count constituents; collinear unsafe
  through the hard-particle energy sharing"). Scope stated (H18/H18v): "do not count" means count at fixed IRC-safe content
  (exact collinear split of soft particles: −0.006 to −0.020 per particle vs LR(N) −0.068). Soft particles that add energy
  flow move them about as much as an IRC-safe EFN. 0.7 → 0.75.
- D10 (wording kept, 0.4): the count response is model/dataset specific (JetClass W-vs-QCD nets 1.2–2.3× LR(N) under exact
  splits), not an effect of the diffuse edit. H18's proposed wording ("D10's count response is not caused by the diffuse
  edit; the e1 contrast excludes an edit difference") is adopted only for the exact-split and c4 − c4m evidence; the e1 part
  is not (H18v: added pT differs 0.13 vs 0.99 GeV). Top LorentzNet's c4 response is about 40 % added four-momentum.
  New observation, unexplained: top taggers (JetClass, Top Landscape) move toward QCD under added particles while LR(N)
  moves toward top.
- D5 0.6 → 0.65 (exact-split count response of JetClass W-vs-QCD nets; QG exception stands).
- D6 (wording kept) 0.7 → 0.75: the Top-trained W-pair response holds on JetClass tops (H19, sample-independent).
- D11 (new, H19v, 0.45): JetClass-trained ParT and ParticleNet also use the W-pair mass when edited jets are rebuilt about
  their own axis. H19's proposed reading ("JetClass nets respond only at 100 GeV; the contrast depends on edit direction",
  confidence 0.15) refuted by H19v (axis-build artefact) and not adopted. D1's ParT extension (H3v null) is contradicted
  pending a rerun of H3v with the same rebuild (H20); D1's JEDI-linear statement unchanged.
- D9 (wording kept, 0.55): scope added: H12's five-class 21–38 % is 67–77 % W/Z jets; on t/q/g jets the top call changes on
  3.6–10.7 % of jets.
- D12 (new, H16/H16v, 0.55): in small taggers boost sensitivity comes from architecture, inputs and task, not the training
  sample; with absolute-pT inputs the sample sets the direction. H16's "every net reads moments about the axis rather than
  the invariant mass" not adopted (H16v TC control).
- D4m 0.5 → 0.45: H16v TC (m_A fixed, centroid moved) changes 4.4–13.8 % of small-net calls vs 1.3–2.8 % for a rotation;
  H19v: JetClass ParT/PN respond to an axis shift alone. D4 (0.8, =) gains an instance (H19's fixed-axis build).

**Thesis log (round D5, 2026-10-20, nodes H23, H24, H25).** v1 thesis wording kept. The earlier W-pair and soft-contamination
edits were audited with every edited jet rebuilt about its own axis (H23, absorbs H20); the Herwig-trained QG models were
evaluated (H24, absorbs H15); the prong-shape variable τ32 was tested at fixed m and mW (H25).
- D1 (v1 wording kept: "JEDI: mass windows plus a ΣpT shortcut; no W-mass constraint inside top"; JEDI-linear part
  unchanged, 0.85). The ParT/JetClass extension added in round D1 from H3v ("neither JEDI-linear nor ParT_full nor ParT_kin
  responds to the W-pair mass inside top beyond a W-fixed control", 2026-10-19) is withdrawn (2026-10-20, H23/H23v): with
  each edited jet rebuilt about its own axis, H3v's own k-means edit gives ParT_full 1.93× [1.51, 2.39] MLPx at 60 GeV
  (Holm 1.9e-5 on fresh rows; = 1.07× MLPxW), ParT_kin and PN_kin 2.7–3.0×, and at 100 GeV ParT_full −0.42 [−0.63, −0.22]
  beyond MLPxW. The H3v null came mostly from the fixed-axis build. H23's own wording "at 100 GeV no model responds beyond
  MLPx" and "ParT_full not separated from MLPx after Holm" were not reproduced by H23v and are not adopted.
- D11 (wording of 2026-10-20 kept: "JetClass-trained ParT and ParticleNet ... use the W-candidate pair mass ... at the level
  of Top-Landscape-trained models, when each edited jet's inputs are computed about its own axis"; its quantitative clause
  "3–6× a classifier that sees only the edit's side effects" refined): rc T60 is 2.9–4.5× MLPx and 0.8–1.25× MLPxW (a
  reference given the pair mass) on two independent samples; T100 4.3–6.3× MLPx (1.4–2.1× MLPxW); the response is ordered in
  |mW − 80| (60 > 70, 100 > 90 GeV, Holm ≤ 4e-8 for all four nets). 0.45 → 0.6. H23's "for ParT the translation response
  equals the fixed-minus-recentred difference within ±0.15" holds for ParT_kin only (H23v: ParT_full residual +0.48, PN
  +0.7 to +1.2); the source of the full models' extra non-W-control response is not identified (feature zeroing is
  off-distribution; H28).
- D6 (wording kept, 0.75): Top-trained networks on Top data are unchanged by the audit (their original builds already
  recomputed the axis; rc Top ParT T60 −1.73 vs H4v −1.79; not re-run by the verifier).
- D13 (new, H25/H25v, 0.5): top taggers (Top ParT, PN, LorentzNet, PFN, EFN; JetClass ParT_kin, PN_kin) use the radiation
  pattern inside the three prongs beyond m and mW, but do not follow τ32 as a single number. H25's proposed reading ("these
  taggers use prong-shape information beyond m and mW, comparable to a τ32 classifier", thesis note "top taggers use prong
  shape (τ32 / subjet width)") is adopted only for the S edit's existence of a response; the τ32 attribution is not
  supported (H25v profile edit at fixed subjet masses: 26 of 46 significant cells with the τ32 sign, 20 opposite). The
  pre-registered edit (subjet four-momenta kept) is infeasible.
- D7 (wording of 2026-10-19 kept; scope of 2026-10-20 round D4 kept) 0.75 → 0.8: the no-counting result holds for ParT_kin
  and PN_kin trained on Herwig 7.1 as well as Pythia (H24/H24v; split − ΔN = 0 control −0.008 to +0.009 per particle vs
  LR(N) +0.08 to +0.105 on both test generators); the Pythia-vs-Herwig gluon output gap is not carried by multiplicity
  (N-swap reproduces ≤ 6 % of it vs 65–69 % for GBDT(EFP+N)). H1 (training generator sets the strategy, sign following
  LR(N)) excluded. Refinement: Herwig training shifts the count response by −0.01 to −0.03 per particle (beyond the
  checkpoint spread; seed spread unmeasured, H26); removing the softest particles at fixed N moves all four nets toward quark
  (−0.045 to −0.056 per particle), so the soft particles' momenta and positions are used. H24's "at most a tenth of LR(N)"
  holds toward gluon only (Herwig PN moves toward quark by up to a third of LR(N)'s size).
- D4m (0.45, =): instance added, not verified: Top ParT/PN fed edited jets on a fixed axis respond to a pure translation
  (dTN −1.1 to −2.9 log-odds), as the JetClass nets do (H23).
- D10 (0.4, =): H13's pu-arm call changes differ ≤ 1.5 pp between fixed-axis and recentred builds (H23; not re-run by the
  verifier).
- Methods rule (H23): any edit that moves the E-scheme axis is rebuilt about the new axis before its effect is attributed to
  a physical quantity; results computed on a fixed axis are reported as such.

**Thesis log (round D6, 2026-10-20, nodes H29, H30, H31).** v1 thesis wording kept. The JEDI-linear W-mass null was retested
under the rebuild rule (H29); the QG soft-emission response was mapped over the Lund plane (H30, exploratory); the top
Dalitz position was tested at smoke scale (H31, exploratory, full run blocked).
- D1 (v1 wording kept: "JEDI: mass windows plus a ΣpT shortcut; no W-mass constraint inside top"; scope since round D5:
  JEDI-linear). Quantitative clause added (2026-10-20, H29/H29v): "rebuilt about each edited jet's own axis, JEDI-linear's
  response to moving the W-candidate pair mass at fixed m and τ32 lies inside the range of retrained classifiers given only
  the edit's side effects at 60, 70 and 100 GeV, and below every classifier given the pair mass at 100 GeV (0.23–0.65×)".
  H29's proposed wording ("equals a side-effect classifier without the pair mass (1.01× at 60 GeV) and is 0.40–0.42× one with
  it") is not adopted as point ratios: H29v shows a ±50 % spread across reference retrains (0.57–1.76× MLPx members,
  0.29–0.99× MLPxW members at 60 GeV). Exception recorded: at 90 GeV JEDI-linear's top score rises (+0.15 on two samples)
  while all 12 references fall. The fixed-axis H4-type numbers (+0.92) are the translation response and are not cited.
  0.85 → 0.8 (verifier 0.6; weak hls4ml positive control, MLPx and MLPxW AUC both 0.951).
- D4 (0.8, =): instance added (H29): on a fixed axis, the non-W control's 0.076 rad axis shift moves JEDI-linear by −1.10
  log-odds. D4m (0.45, =): consistent with the axis-relative m_A (A4); not a test of the Δ-sign rule.
- D7 (wording kept, 0.8, =). H30's reading ("for kt up to ~2 GeV the networks follow the Lund-density ratio / pT^D and not
  girth; Herwig- vs Pythia-trained maps differ at kt > 1 GeV, not in the hadronisation region") is refuted in its attribution
  by H30v (0.3) and not adopted: the edit transferred up to ~50 GeV from the hardest particle, and that transfer carries the
  small-angle rise (pT-only +0.24, angle at fixed pT −0.14; absent on Herwig jets); the generator-difference location
  depends on normalisation and there is one seed per generator. Retained as an observation: moving one particle at fixed pT
  changes the output, and a 25–40 GeV particle placed at wide angle (kt 7.5 GeV) moves ParT toward quark (−0.40) while every
  classical reference moves toward gluon. No new D claim.
- D6, D11, D13 (=): H31 smoke (150 jets per class) gives the H1 sign on JetClass tops (nine taggers −0.83 to −1.47 vs MLP5
  −0.64) but a side-effect classifier moves as much (−0.68) and no test passes Holm; full run pending (H32). D11
  cross-model note (H29, not verified): hls4ml PFNs respond at 1.7–1.8× MLPx at 60 GeV vs 2.9–4.5× for JetClass ParT/PN.
- Methods rule added (H29v): ratios to reference classifiers are quoted as ranges across reference retrains, not single fits.

**Thesis log (round D7, 2026-10-20, nodes H32, H35, H36).** v1 thesis wording kept. The W decay angle inside top was run at
full statistics with a side-effect-matched control (H32); the location of displaced tracks among a top jet's subjets was
tested at fixed displaced-track count (H35, exploratory); axis dependence was measured on Top Landscape and quark–gluon
taggers with a pre-registered prediction and LorentzNet as zero (H36, exploratory).
- New claim D15 (0.4): "top taggers respond to the W decay angle |cos θ*| at fixed m_top and m_W beyond the τ32 and
  prong-pT-fraction side effects: clearly for PFN and EFN on both datasets, and for Top ParT, LorentzNet, JetClass
  ParT_kin / PN_kin on JetClass tops in a τ32-matched subset". H32's proposed wording ("decay-angle response mediated
  mainly by τ32 / prong sharing; angle-specific for PFN / EFN") is refuted by H32v (0.3) and not adopted: the negative
  pooled b_cos of the non-Deep-Sets networks is a difference between edit directions, and on matched JetClass jets these
  networks drop 0.3–0.7 log-odds where the angle-blind MLPx stays within 0.09.
- New claim D14 (0.45): "at fixed jet-level displaced-track count, JetClass ParT_full / PN_full use which subjet carries the
  displacement; the response follows the subjet that already holds most displaced tracks, not the mass-assigned b-prong".
  H35's proposed wording ("b-prong localisation relative to the three-prong mass structure, as a subjet b-tag does, 2–3×
  the per-subjet-count classifier") is refuted by H35v (0.3) and not adopted: with a size- and pT-matched control the
  contrast is −1.72 / −1.06 (not −2.94 / −2.09), and the reverse move onto the mass-assigned b-prong also lowers the score.
  D2 (0.75, =) refined: displacement is used per subjet as well as by multiplicity.
- D4 (0.8, =): scope extended to Top Landscape and QG (H36/H36v, 0.7): 3–25 % of calls change about WTA / HP; LorentzNet,
  LR(N), LR(m_SD, τ32) are zero by construction. H36's "no evidence that the networks read the axis beyond a translation"
  is replaced by H36v's statement: the axis change is exactly a translation for these inputs, and the direction-specific
  part lies within the weight-permuted-network band.
- D4m 0.45 → 0.4: on Top and QG the call change follows each model's generic translation sensitivity; the
  quantitative prediction method is biased (R/pred 0.57–1.22).
- D6, D11, D13 (=): notes added; the τ32 change of a rigid W-decay rotation cannot be reproduced by any rigid control at
  fixed |cos θ*| (42–59 %), so D13 (prong pattern) and D15 (decay angle) are not fully separated.
- No new dataset. Proposed: H37 (τ32-complete control for D15), H38 (cause of the within-prong displacement response;
  b vs charm), H39 (architecture-matched nulls and seeds for the axis-direction test).

**Thesis log (round D8, 2026-10-20, nodes H39, H40, H41).** v1 thesis wording kept. Two new physics quantities tested at
fixed kinematics: particle species and jet charge in quark–gluon taggers (H40, exploratory) and colour flow in W/Z/H jets
(H41, exploratory); the axis-direction test of D4m was run against architecture-matched untrained nulls (H39).
- New claim D16 (0.5): "QG ParT / PN kinpid use the sign of the jet charge (−0.25 / −0.23 log-odds × sign Q_0.3 under
  charge conjugation, ≈ 0.85 of LR(Q_0.3)) and the neutral-species label of soft particles (γ quark-ward, K0L gluon-ward
  at fixed charged fraction); K/p vs π is not in their inputs". H40's proposed wording is kept except for one statement:
  "PN does not respond to the hardest-charged flip (+0.002)" is refuted by H40v (unsigned average of a sign-following
  response; signed −0.41, as ParT). The size of the species response is within the permuted-network range for PN; only
  its direction separates trained from null. The u-quark (Z+q) origin of the signed-Q use is an interpretation. New
  taxonomy item for Paper D: a sample-specific flavour prior (charge sign), distinct from quark/gluon radiation physics.
- New claim D17 (0.5): "JetClass ParT / PN (full and kin) use where soft radiation sits relative to the two prongs of
  W/Z/H jets, at fixed m, τ21, girth and D2 (5 %); near-to-far and far-to-near moves act in opposite directions; the QCD
  reverse direction is not resolved". H41's "1.1–7× a pull-angle classifier" not adopted (H41v: response ratios between
  classifiers of different AUC do not measure relative use); H41's shape-confound caveat (D2 not held) answered by H41v.
- D4m (0.4, =): H39 (not verified) finds the direction part of trained Top / QG networks inside the untrained same-
  architecture spread in all 42 cells, with a working-point-dependent sign and a direction effect already present in
  untrained EFNs. This weakens the Top/QG extension of D4m; the confidence is held until the seed-sign arm (running) and
  a verifier report arrive. D4 (0.8, =): H36 call changes reproduced.
- Proposed: H42 (shuffled-label / second-seed QG kinpid null and C2ST for D16; Z+q vs dijet-like flavour split of the
  charge response), H43 (QCD reverse direction for D17 at larger N; 2 % shape match; on-manifold colour-flow edit; Top
  arm rerun), H39 rerun as d-h39-run2 when the s1/shuf trainings finish, followed by its verifier.

**Thesis log (round D9, 2026-10-20, nodes H44, H45, H42).** v1 thesis wording kept. Two classical quantities tested at
fixed kinematics across models: the impact-parameter size and displaced-system mass for b vs c (H44, exploratory) and the
two-prong momentum sharing at exactly fixed jet four-momentum on two datasets (H45, exploratory); the cause of D16 measured
(H42 part 1). No new dataset (H44 adds the JetClass HToCC test file; H45 uses existing hls4ml and JetClass samples).
- New claim D19 (0.4): "at fixed displaced-track counts above every S threshold, JetClass ParT_full / PN_full respond to
  impact-parameter values in the bb-vs-cc score; about half of it is direction-independent (equal-size random edit), prompt
  tracks matter as much, lifetime use not established, vertex mass unresolved". H44's proposed wording ("they use how far
  the displaced tracks are, in the direction of the longer B-hadron lifetime; D2 'multiplicity, not lifetime' holds for bb
  vs QCD but not for bb vs cc") is refuted in part by H44v (0.35) and not adopted: the random-direction control Lr moves bb
  jets as far as Lc(0.4); in JetClass at fixed counts b-jet tracks are less displaced than c-jet tracks (max ln S −0.99),
  opposite to the network direction; the bb-vs-QCD count-only statement holds only for the displaced edit on bb jets.
- D2 (0.75, =): wording kept ("ParT: displaced-track multiplicity, not lifetime; ..."), scope note added (see the claim-
  confidence row): count-only for bb vs QCD on H→bb jets; impact-parameter values used beyond counts for bb vs cc (D19).
- New claim D18 (0.5): "two-prong taggers on JetClass and hls4ml respond to the prong momentum-sharing configuration at
  exactly fixed jet four-momentum, balanced prongs more W/Z-like; same-angle prong-fixed rotation ≤ 0.17; z use vs ΔR / D2 /
  prong masses not established". H45's readings on W-vs-Z ("reduces separation in both directions") and on q/g jets are
  corrected by H45v (the JetClass W-vs-Z score follows dc in the same direction for W and Z jets; hls4ml q/g not uniform).
- D1 (0.8, =) refined: JEDI-linear's W/Z score responds to D18's edit at fixed m_A and ΣpT (< 1 %), so on W/Z jets it
  reads more than mass windows plus ΣpT; the mechanism is loop A/B's (handoff).
- D16 0.5 → 0.55 (H42 part 1, not verified): quark-jet charge asymmetry measured on both generators, E1 on-manifold for
  gluon jets, network rule not adapted to Herwig; species relabels off-manifold. Seed / shuffled-label null running.
- New taxonomy item: "edit-pattern sensitivity" — a response to any change of a feature's per-particle pattern at equal
  size (Lr, cn), which a direction-specific reading must exceed.
- Running: d-h42-qg-{part,pn}-kp-{s1,shuf} (H42 null), d-h39-{top,qg}-ef, d-h39-{top,qg}-pn-{s1,shuf}, d-h39-qg-part-s1
  (H39 seed/shuffle). Proposed: H46 (lifetime-relation IP edit vs relation-breaking control; neutral V exchange control),
  H47 (z at fixed ΔR and prong masses; reference with reclustered prong masses; JetClass vs hls4ml W-vs-Z difference).

**Thesis log (round D10, 2026-10-20, nodes H48, H49, H50).** v1 thesis wording kept. Three classical quantities tested
across models: the visible-mass endpoints of leptonic decays (t→bℓν m_bl ≤ 152.6 GeV; H→ℓνqq′ m_vis ≤ 125 GeV; H48,
exploratory), the soft-drop (groomed) mass against the plain mass at matched soft contamination on all three two-prong /
top datasets (H49, exploratory), and z vs ΔR vs reclustered prong masses for D18 (H50 = proposed H47). All three verifiers
refuted the worker's reading in part (confidence 0.3 each); the corrected readings are adopted. No new dataset.
- New claim D20 (0.35): "in H→ℓνqq′ jets the networks respond to the lepton's opening angle to the other particles beyond
  mass and isolation, most strongly at the 125 GeV crossing; the same visible-mass rise made by moving a hadron gives no
  excess, so the edge as such is not read; no two-way step at the t→bℓν endpoint". H48's proposed wording ("the networks
  use the H→ℓνqq′ visible-mass edge"; "consistent with use of the m_bl endpoint for kin models") is refuted by H48v's
  hadron-edit control (ParT_full excess −0.12 [−0.34, +0.12]) and not adopted.
- New claim D21 (0.4): "hls4ml W/Z taggers follow the plain mass, not m_SD, at matched count, ΣpT and Δm; JetClass and
  Top taggers respond strongly to groomed-away soft particles and, to kept ones, with about 0.16–0.45 of an m_SD
  classifier's per-jet response plus a direction-independent penalty". H49's proposed readings ("JetClass W/Z add a
  groomed-like response, r_SD 0.4–1.4"; "top taggers respond opposite to the m_SD references") are refuted by H49v's
  per-jet direction test and not adopted.
- D18 (0.5, =): wording kept; open clause narrowed by H50/H50v: the asymmetric-edit response is not a reclustered
  prong-mass effect (r_A 1.08 / 1.10); z vs (ΔR, m, prong masses) is not separable at fixed prong masses (a z-blind,
  D2-blind reference reproduces arm C within 0.16); H50's "H0_dR excluded" is refuted by H50v and not adopted.
- D10 (0.4, =): scoped by H49 — the JetClass W/Z response under soft contamination depends on where the particles sit
  (wide-angle G_out −1.25 to −1.54 vs count-only c0 −0.01 to −0.54), so "mainly the number of added particles" is not
  sufficient for JetClass W/Z.
- D1 (0.8, =): supported by H49v (JEDI-linear plain-mass reading on W/Z; b ≤ 0.03). D2 (0.75, =): refined by D20.
- Methods: reference ratios are now reported with the per-jet slope b and offset a (D = a + b·D_ref), and reference refits
  are counted in the uncertainty (H49v r_SD 1.15 → 1.52; H48v MLP(m) step −0.56 → −0.95).
- Running (not analysed this round): d-h39-qg-ef, d-h39-qg-part-s1, d-h39-qg-pn-shuf, d-h39-top-pn-s1,
  d-h39-top-pn-shuf (H39); d-h42-qg-part-kp-s1, d-h42-qg-part-kp-shuf, d-h42-qg-pn-kp-s1 (H42). d-h42-qg-pn-kp-shuf
  Failed again (not relaunched this round). Proposed: H51 (lepton–quark angle at fixed isolation; pair term 2 p_ℓ·p_B;
  Δm scan; C2ST), H52 (source of the kept-particle penalty; placement scan), H53 (z at fixed D2; z-decorrelated D2 reference).

**Thesis log (round D11, 2026-10-21, nodes H54, H55, H56).** v1 thesis wording kept. First generator-level intervention in the
project: the KMT quark/gluon sample regenerated with Pythia 8.317 and only the hadronisation (or shower) settings changed
(H54, exploratory); a classical-tagger fingerprint panel over all four datasets at published working points (H55,
exploratory); second-seed and untrained-network nulls for the axis-direction and count results (H56). New dataset
content: generated Pythia QG jets (V0, Hz±, S±; /j-jepa-vol/aaron-interp/data/qg/gen).
- D7 v1 wording (2026-10-19, kept: "quark–gluon taggers (ParT, ParticleNet, LorentzNet) do not count constituents; they are
  collinear unsafe through the energy sharing of the hardest particles") is weakened by H54/H54v (verifier 0.8). Under a
  hadronisation-only change that moves N by about ±11 % with IRC-safe shapes within 0.6 % and the EFN within 0.07, every
  Pythia-trained non-IRC-safe network moves toward the counting references (Hz+ ParT_kin −0.136 / −0.215 log-odds vs EFN
  +0.025 / −0.004, LR(N) −0.42; H0 "network = EFN" excluded, Holm p < 1e-8; σ-only control, where the EFN moves the other
  way, excludes it too). D7 v2 (2026-10-21): "under hand-made edits at fixed IRC-safe content (exact collinear splits) QG
  taggers respond to N at 3.5–8 % of a count-only LR(N), reproducibly across training seeds (H56/H56v); the reviewer form
  'no response to N beyond IRC-safe energy flow' holds for these edits only". 0.8 → 0.75 (v2 scope).
- New claim D22 (0.7, H54/H54v): "under generator-level hadronisation changes Pythia-trained QG ParT, ParticleNet,
  LorentzNet and PFN respond beyond IRC-safe energy flow, in the direction of a count classifier: kinematic ParT /
  ParticleNet 0.30–0.65 of the count increment (reference-dependent), particle-ID models and LorentzNet 0.44–1.02, PFN
  0.41–0.80; the response is a weighted mixture of the EFN's and LR(N)'s (α 0.46–0.78, β 0.33–0.98 over 30 settings);
  count vs soft-hadron momentum (pT^D) not separated; Herwig-trained networks respond less (β ≈ 0–0.24)". H54's wording
  "about half" for the kinematic networks is replaced by "a third to two-thirds" (H54v); H54's "H1 not excluded for
  ParticleNet_kinpid on gluon jets" depends on the criterion (κ CI [0.90, 0.98] excludes 1; Holm test p 0.28). The shower
  α_s arm does not discriminate (EFN and LR(N) predictions within 0.05).
- New claim D23 (0.3, H55/H55v, corrected reading): "under one nine-edit library, JetClass W/Z/top and Top Landscape networks
  change calls in a pattern nearest m_SD + τ21 / D2 / τ32 taggers, not the plain mass window; hls4ml networks are nearest
  the plain mass window for Z (all five) and top (JEDI-linear, Linformer); groomed mass vs shape not separated". H55's
  proposed wording ("on W/Z/top every network is nearest m_SD + shape; above the permuted null on JetClass W/Z and hls4ml Z;
  axis and split components no classical tagger has", 0.5) is refuted by H55v and not adopted: wrong on hls4ml; Holm
  p-values from a normal approximation to 6–30 permuted copies (exact rank p cannot pass Holm); LR(girth) changes 7–12 %
  under axis changes and LR(N) 4–10 % under splits; QG axis responses 0–4 % (EFN 18 %). Pre-registered pairings (JEDI-linear
  with the plain mass window; QG nets with girth): the first holds for hls4ml Z / top only, the second not observed (QG nets
  nearest LR(N, pT^D)). Fresh rerun not completed (H58).
- D4m 0.4 → 0.35 (H56/H56v, verifier 0.6): with a second training seed and 12–20 untrained networks per architecture, the
  direction part of the Top / QG axis response is inside the untrained distribution in all 82 tests (min uncorrected p
  0.078); seed differences of 1–2 pp exist at the sign call; a learned direction effect below about two untrained SDs
  (0.6–9 pp) is not excluded. hls4ml / JetClass evidence (X7/X7v) unchanged.
- D5 (0.65, =): H55 adds four-dataset instances (networks 1–5 % of calls under exact collinear splits, IRC-safe classical
  taggers 0 %, EFN 0 %). D21 / D1 (=): H55v hls4ml nearest-mass-window result agrees. D16 (0.55, =): charge null not run
  (kinpid seed-1 / shuffled trainings OOMKilled at 40 GiB; relaunched at 48 Gi). H26 answered for Pythia seeds: the
  Herwig − Pythia count-response shift is 4–5× (ParT_kin) and ≥ 8× (PN_kin) the seed difference.
- Methods: shuffled-label trainings with hash labels collapse to a constant output and give no spread (not a usable null);
  jet-bootstrap intervals at fixed-efficiency thresholds understate between-sample spread (H56v: the same network's D moves
  1.0 pp between samples); nulls with fewer than ~20 copies are reported with exact rank p, not a normal approximation.
- Running: d-h56-qg-{part,pn}-kp-{s1,shuf} (D16 null), d-h39-qg-pn-shuf. Proposed: H57 (count vs soft-hadron momentum
  on-manifold; seeds for the H54 networks), H58 (complete H55v rerun with an m_SD-only tagger and exact nulls), H59 (D16
  charge null on the retrained kinpid nets; a non-collapsing label null).

**Thesis log (round D12, 2026-10-21, nodes H57, H58, H60).** v1 thesis wording kept. Second and third generator-level
interventions: Pythia hadronisation and hadron-decay settings that move the particle count N and the momentum sharing pT^D
apart on quark/gluon jets (H57, exploratory), and the H54 hadronisation change applied to generated top and QCD jets (H60,
exploratory, first top-tagging generator test); the H55 classical-tagger fingerprint rerun at matched working points (H58).
New dataset content: generated particle-level Pythia top / QCD jets (data/top/gen_h60) and H57 QG settings (data/qg/gen/h57).
- D22 v1 (0.7, kept: "… shift beyond the IRC-safe EFN, in the direction of a count classifier …") → D22 v2 (0.65, H57/H57v,
  verifier 0.5): "the beyond-EFN response of Pythia-trained ParT_kin / PN_kin is not a response to the total count N; it
  tracks fragmentation hardness, measured equally well by pT^D and by the count of hadrons above 1–4 GeV". At fixed pT^D a
  7 % count drop gives κ_N 0.19–0.28 (quark) and 0.04–0.11 (gluon) on two event samples (H0_N predicted 0.5–0.9); under PA−
  (π⁰ stable, softer string) ParT_kin moves gluon-ward with LR(pT^D) (−0.10 / −0.23 vs −0.13 / −0.20) while LR(N) moves
  quark-ward. H57's "not how many there are" corrected by H57v to "not the total number": the hard-hadron count N(pT > 1–4
  GeV) fits as well as pT^D (Δs correlation 0.97–0.99; ParT gluon χ² 49 vs 107). PA− is off-manifold (C2ST 0.84–0.90) and a
  cancellation of two parts; the π⁰-merge part moves ParT 2–5× more than LR(pT^D) predicts beyond the EFN. PFN's extra
  count part is a count above about 1 GeV. Lowered 0.7 → 0.65 because the new part rests on hand-picked off-manifold settings.
- New claim D24 (0.5, H60/H60v, verifier 0.6): at particle level, Top Landscape and JetClass top taggers call top jets less
  top-like when hadronisation alone adds about 9 % particles at fixed m, τ32 and mW (ParT −0.44 [−0.49, −0.38], EFN +0.01),
  with the sign of GBDT(EFP+N) (κ 0.7–1.2 for ParT / PN-type nets), not LR(N); count-only split edits reproduce 0.75–0.84
  of it and merges remove it. This gives the H18/H21 observation ("top taggers move toward QCD under added particles while
  LR(N) moves toward top") a mechanism: extra particles at fixed IRC-safe substructure are QCD-like. H60's efficiency
  statement (ParT ε_S 0.30 → 0.24) is particle-level only: under a calorimeter-like proxy ParT moves −0.12 and JetClass
  ParT_kin does not move (+0.007). QCD-jet responses are architecture dependent and not count responses (H60v). Not
  claimed: the detector-level size (H61).
- D23 v1 (0.3, kept: "… nearest m_SD + τ21 / D2 / τ32 taggers at published cuts …") weakened by H58: at matched 50 %
  efficiency the shape preference vanishes (published-cut preference is a working-point effect). H58's narrowed wording
  ("top taggers follow the groomed, not the ungroomed, mass", proposed 0.55) refuted by H58v (verifier 0.2) and not adopted:
  the groomed-over-ungroomed preference comes from the decay-angle rotation at fixed mass alone (without it d_groom −0.01 to
  +0.03), and top networks still change 0.40–0.60 as many calls under groomed-away soft particles as under kept ones. D23 v2
  (0.4, verifier's reading): top taggers respond to prong-sharing changes at fixed mass like m_SD / τ32 taggers, are partly
  insensitive to groomed-away radiation (r_g 0.40–0.60), and hls4ml W/Z networks respond to both alike (r_g 1.00). H58's
  "hls4ml split by architecture" is a weighting effect of the decay and gout columns, not seen on r_g.
- D7 (0.75, =): "do not follow the total count" supported for ParT / ParticleNet on generator-level QG jets (H57/H57v).
  D5 (0.65, =): top-tagging split instance (H60v). D21 (0.4, =): r_g instances (H58v). D1–D4 (=): no test this round.
- Methods: a generator-variant comparison is checked against competing single observables that move with the tested one
  (H57v: N vs N(pT > x) vs pT^D, χ² over all settings); particle-level variants are checked under a detector proxy before a
  size statement (H60v); fingerprint contrasts are reported with leave-one-edit-out magnitudes and at a matched window and
  efficiency (H58v); Herwig 7 is not available on the cluster (Herwig-side knobs not run).
- Running: d-h56-qg-pn-kp-s1, d-h56-qg-pn-kp-shuf (D16 null; ParT kinpid s1 / shuf and d-h39-qg-pn-shuf finished).
  Proposed: H59 (D16 charge null when the PN trainings finish), H61 (Delphes, MPI-off top variants), H62 (pT^D vs hard-hadron
  count; on-manifold PA-type setting; seeds), H63 (which prong-sharing quantity top taggers follow under the decay rotation;
  r_g dose-response; edit weighting).
